[2]^n (binary) ops in parallel with [3]^n (ternary) ops, [2|2]^n quaternary ops (orthogonal binary pairs), [5]^n (quinternary), etc? cooool</p></div></div></div></div><div data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ <span class="hljs-built_in">cd</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/pli &amp;&amp; sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'/^@module llama_pool/,/^}/p'</span> a9nn.pli | <span class="hljs-built_in">head</span> -45; <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> <span class="hljs-string">"=== driver ==="</span>; sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'/^@module echobeats_driver/,/^}/p'</span> a9nn.pli
@module llama_pool(num_instances, base_port)
{
    @initial
    {
        [llamapool
            poolcfg{num_instances, base_port}
            <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> (k = 1; k &lt;= num_instances; k++)
            {
                [llama{k}
                    port{base_port + k - 1}
                    load{0}
                    tokens_served{0}
                ]<span class="hljs-string">'llamapool
            }
        ]'</span>agent
    }

    @rules reason
    {
        /* Dispatch the prompt to the least-loaded instance: the guard picks
         * the instance whose load is minimal among the pool. */
        [pc{6}, prompt{P}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent
        [load{n}]'</span>llama{k} -&gt;
            [pc{6}, prompt{P}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent
            [load{n + 1}]'</span>llama{k}
            <span class="hljs-built_in">where</span> forall_min load{k}

        /* Stub-mode completion: <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> the prompt, count tokens. */
        [load{n}, tokens_served{t}]<span class="hljs-string">'llama{k} [prompt{P}]'</span>agent -&gt;
            [load{n - 1}, tokens_served{t + 64}]<span class="hljs-string">'llama{k}
            [llama_resp{P, 64, k}]'</span>agent      /* text, tokens, instance <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> */

        [pc{6}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt; [pc{7}]'</span>agent
    }
}
=== driver ===
@module echobeats_driver
{
    @rules beat_handoff
    {
        /* PERCEIVE -&gt; FILTER */
        [pc{1}, raw_input{U}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt; [pc{2}, reg{r0, U}]'</span>agent

        /* FILTER -&gt; RESONATE (attenuate sub-threshold features) */
        [pc{2}, reg{r0, U}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt; [pc{3}, filtered{U}]'</span>agent

        /* RESONATE handled by echo_reservoir (3 -&gt; 4).
         * ENCODE: compress reservoir state into context register R2. */
        [pc{4}, reg{r1, S}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt; [pc{5}, reg{r2, S}]'</span>agent

        /* RECALL (5-&gt;6), REASON (6-&gt;7), EMOTE (7-&gt;8), PLAN (8-&gt;9)
         * are advanced by their own modules above. */

        /* LEARN: store the transition, emit a loss estimate. */
        [pc{9}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt; [pc{10}, loss{0}]'</span>agent

        /* REFLECT (10-&gt;11) by consciousness. */

        /* EXPRESS: package the output (LLaMA text <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> present). */
        [pc{11}, llama_resp{P, T, K}, best_action{A}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt;
            [pc{12}, output{P, A}]'</span>agent

        /* INTEGRATE: write an EpisodeNode to the AtomSpace, bump cycle,
         * and reset PC to 1 <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> the next EchoBeats cycle. */
        [pc{12}, cycle{n}, loss{lo}]<span class="hljs-string">'agent -&gt;
            [pc{1}, cycle{n + 1}]'</span>agent
            (episode_node{n + 1, max(0, 100 - lo)}, out)
    }
}
&lt;shellId: 0 completed with <span class="hljs-built_in">exit</span> code 0&gt;
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"description"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"Insert Phase-4 todos"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">"query"</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">"INSERT INTO todos (id, title, status) VALUES\n ('c1','Map EchoBeats beat-handoff + subsystem hooks for unification','done'),\n ('c2','Write lang/pli/unified.pli (cognitive cycle: REASON&lt;-PLN, EMOTE&lt;-OpenPsi, RECALL/INTEGRATE&lt;-AtomSpace, LEARN&lt;-loss, REFLECT&lt;-layer)','in_progress'),\n ('c3','Write lang/pli/topology.pli (arity-basis membranes [2]^n,[3]^n,[2|2]^n,[5]^n; Matula/prime-power indexing; partition-function selection; elementary differentials via product/chain rules)','pending'),\n ('c4','Add unified + topology tests to test_extensions.pli','pending'),\n ('c5','Add unified-cycle + mixed-radix parallelism demos to demo.pli','pending'),\n ('c6','Register unified/topology in integration.pli','pending'),\n ('c7','Update README (sections, counts, categorical-topology note)','pending'),\n ('c8','validate.sh + secret scan + codeql + commit/push + memory','pending');"</span>

tmp-p-3" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 4 — Unified Cognitive Cycle + Membrane Arity Topology (v2.5)</h2>
<p><strong>Part A — Unify the cognitive cycle</strong> (<code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/unified.pli</code>): wire PLN + OpenPsi + AtomSpace into the a9nn EchoBeats loop so the agent <em>reasons</em> and <em>wants</em>, not just reacts. REASON←PLN forward-chain, EMOTE←OpenPsi modulators, RECALL/INTEGRATE↔AtomSpace, LEARN←loss, REFLECT←consciousness layer. (Equivalent of ReZorg <code node="[object Object]">opencog_unified_agi.pli</code>.)</p>
<p><strong>Part B — Membrane arity topology</strong> (<code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/topology.pli</code>): make concrete the insight that membrane nesting depth = arity of a categorical logic, and that mixed-radix bases enable heterogeneous parallel ops:</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li node="[object Object]">arity-basis membranes: <code node="[object Object]">[2]^n</code> binary, <code node="[object Object]">[3]^n</code> ternary, <code node="[object Object]">[2|2]^n</code> orthogonal binary pairs (quaternionic), <code node="[object Object]">[5]^n</code> quinternary — same shape, different radix, all maximally parallel in one step</li>
<li node="[object Object]">Matula/prime-power indexing of membrane trees (rooted-tree ↔ prime-factorisation bijection)</li>
<li node="[object Object]">partition-function ("natural selection" as root selection) over free hyper-multisets</li>
<li node="[object Object]">elementary differentials via branching product rule + nesting chain rule (B-series flavour)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> tests + demos (unified cycle, mixed-radix parallel ops) + registry + README</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> validate.sh; secret scan; CodeQL; commit/push; memory</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: integers ×100. No change to shared XOR fixture.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">No changes to commit. Pushed existing commits.

Progress<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span>
## Phase <span class="hljs-number">4</span> — Unified Cognitive Cycle + Membrane Arity Topology (v2<span class="hljs-number">.5</span>)

**Part A — Unify the cognitive cycle** (`lang/pli/unified.pli`)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> wire PLN + OpenPsi + AtomSpace into the a9nn EchoBeats loop so the agent *reasons* and *wants*<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> not just reacts. REASON←PLN forward-chain<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> EMOTE←OpenPsi modulators<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> RECALL/INTEGRATE↔AtomSpace<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> LEARN←loss<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> REFLECT←consciousness layer. (Equivalent of ReZorg `opencog_unified_agi.pli`.)

**Part B — Membrane arity topology** (`lang/pli/topology.pli`)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> make concrete the insight that membrane nesting depth = arity of a categorical logic<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> and that mixed-radix bases enable heterogeneous parallel ops<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span>
- arity-basis membranes<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> `<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">2</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n` binary<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">3</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n` ternary<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">2</span>|<span class="hljs-number">2</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n` orthogonal binary pairs (quaternionic)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">5</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n` quinternary — same shape<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> different radix<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> all maximally parallel in one step
- Matula/prime-power indexing of membrane trees (rooted-tree ↔ prime-factorisation bijection)
- partition-function (<span class="hljs-string">"natural selection"</span> as root selection) over free hyper-multisets
- elementary differentials via branching product rule + nesting chain rule (B-series flavour)
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> tests + demos (unified cycle<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> mixed-radix parallel ops) + registry + README
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> validate.sh; secret scan; CodeQL; commit/push; memory

Numerical convention<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> integers ×<span class="hljs-number">100.</span> No change to shared XOR fixture.
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'916,930p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/pli/README.md; <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> <span class="hljs-string">"=== coverage tail ==="</span>; grep -n <span class="hljs-string">"OpenPsi demand urgency"</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/pli/README.md
| `a9nn.pli` | NNECCO cognitive agent: Echo State Reservoir membrane (leaky-integrator neurons, spectral radius), 12-beat EchoBeats loop phase-locked by a PC register, emotion processing unit (8 channels), consciousness layers L0–L3 with loss-driven meta-cognition, prioritised episodic memory, parallel LLaMA pool (least-load antiport dispatch, stub mode), hardware-style registers R0–R4/PC/STA. Mirrors `lang/a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua`. |

<span class="hljs-comment">## Extensions (v2.4)</span>

| Module | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `pln.pli` | Probabilistic Logic Networks over the AtomSpace: deduction, induction, abduction, revision, conjunction, disjunction, negation, modus ponens with SimpleTruthValue strength/confidence formulas; maximally-parallel forward chaining (`pln_forward_chain`). Counterpart of ReZorg/plingua `opencog_pln.pli`. |
| `openpsi.pli` | Dörner Psi motivational system: demands (competence/integrity/exploration/affiliation) with accrual→urgency, goal activation, argmax action selection, satisfaction feedback, and modulators that drive the a9nn `emotion_unit`. Counterpart of ReZorg/plingua `opencog_openpsi.pli`. |

<span class="hljs-comment">### Looking ahead: nD membranes and parallel ledgers</span>

Two directions this port is positioned <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span>:

- **nD generalisation.** 1D/2D/3D spatial models (as <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ReZorg<span class="hljs-string">'s M-Lingua
  `.mli`) generalise to *n-dimensional* membrane arrangements. A future
=== coverage tail ===
640:- ✅ OpenPsi demand urgency, action selection, satisfaction and emotion bridge
&lt;shellId: 6 completed with exit code 0&gt;
</span></code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK"><div class="ToolGroupRenderer-module__toolGroupHeader__zA5xT"><div data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 4 — Unified Cognitive Cycle + Membrane Arity Topology (v2.5)</h2>
<p><strong>Part A — <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/unified.pli</code></strong> (unified cognitive cycle): the a9nn EchoBeats spine with PLN + OpenPsi + AtomSpace overlaid so the agent <em>reasons, wants and remembers</em>.</p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">uni_recall</code> (RECALL←AtomSpace ECAN focus), <code node="[object Object]">uni_reason</code> (REASON←PLN forward chain), <code node="[object Object]">uni_emote</code> (EMOTE←OpenPsi modulators), <code node="[object Object]">uni_learn</code> (LEARN←PLN revision + reward→loss), <code node="[object Object]">uni_integrate</code> (INTEGRATE→persist learned implication atoms)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">unified_agent</code> composite model; counterpart of ReZorg <code node="[object Object]">opencog_unified_agi.pli</code></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Part B — <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/topology.pli</code></strong> (membrane arity topologies): realizes the insight that membrane-tree shape = arity of a categorical logic + mixed-radix parallelism.</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li node="[object Object]">
<p>Mixed-radix bases: <code node="[object Object]">binary_basis</code>/<code node="[object Object]">ternary_basis</code>/<code node="[object Object]">quaternionic_basis</code>/<code node="[object Object]">quinternary_basis</code> (<code node="[object Object]">[2]^n</code>/<code node="[object Object]">[3]^n</code>/<code node="[object Object]">[2|2]^n</code>/<code node="[object Object]">[5]^n</code>) — heterogeneous ops in one maximally-parallel step</p>
</li>
<li node="[object Object]">
<p><code node="[object Object]">matula_index</code> — rooted-tree ↔ prime-factorisation (canonical shape identity)</p>
</li>
<li node="[object Object]">
<p><code node="[object Object]">elementary_differentials</code> — product (<code node="[object Object]">k*r+1</code>) / chain (<code node="[object Object]">r+s</code>) rules (B-series flavour)</p>
</li>
<li node="[object Object]">
<p><code node="[object Object]">partition_selection</code> — partition function over free hyper-multisets as "natural selection = root selection"</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]">
<p><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> 12 tests in <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code> (54 → 66)</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]">
<p><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> 2 demos in <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code> (22 → 24): unified cycle, mixed-radix parallel ops</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]">
<p><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Register <code node="[object Object]">unified</code> + <code node="[object Object]">topology</code> in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code> (v2.5)</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]">
<p><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">README.md</code>: feature bullets, API sections, roadmap, v2.5 table, counts (66 tests / 24 demos), coverage, categorical-topology framing</p>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]">
<p><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code> passes all 29 files; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A (no analyzable languages)</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: integers ×100 (Matula numbers/counts exact). No change to shared XOR fixture — cross-port consistency preserved.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">Committed and pushed<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> feat(pli)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> unified cognitive cycle + membrane arity topology (v2<span class="hljs-number">.5</span>)

## Phase <span class="hljs-number">4</span> — Unified Cognitive Cycle + Membrane Arity Topology (v2<span class="hljs-number">.5</span>)

**Part A — `lang/pli/unified.pli`** (unified cognitive cycle)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> the a9nn EchoBeats spine with PLN + OpenPsi + AtomSpace overlaid so the agent *reasons<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> wants and remembers*.
- `uni_recall` (RECALL←AtomSpace ECAN focus)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `uni_reason` (REASON←PLN forward chain)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `uni_emote` (EMOTE←OpenPsi modulators)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `uni_learn` (LEARN←PLN revision + reward→loss)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> `uni_integrate` (INTEGRATE→persist learned implication atoms)
- `unified_agent` composite model; counterpart of ReZorg `opencog_unified_agi.pli`

**Part B — `lang/pli/topology.pli`** (membrane arity topologies)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> realizes the insight that membrane-tree shape = arity of a categorical logic + mixed-radix parallelism.
- Mixed-radix bases<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> `binary_basis`/`ternary_basis`/`quaternionic_basis`/`quinternary_basis` (`<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">2</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n`/`<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">3</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n`/`<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">2</span>|<span class="hljs-number">2</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n`/`<span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span><span class="hljs-number">5</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span>^n`) — heterogeneous ops in one maximally-parallel step
- `matula_index` — rooted-tree ↔ prime-factorisation (canonical shape identity)
- `elementary_differentials` — product (`k*r+<span class="hljs-number">1</span>`) / chain (`r+s`) rules (B-series flavour)
- `partition_selection` — partition function over free hyper-multisets as <span class="hljs-string">"natural selection = root selection"</span>

- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span>x<span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> <span class="hljs-number">12</span> tests in `test_extensions.pli` (<span class="hljs-number">54</span> → <span class="hljs-number">66</span>)

<p>1 row(s) updated.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="CopilotMessageBubble-module__container__oW7JC"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf CopilotMessageBubble-module__text__ADukm" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><p>Phase 4 complete ✅ — both the unified cognitive cycle and the arity-topology module are built, tested, documented, and pushed.</p>
<h2>Part A — <code node="[object Object]">unified.pli</code>: the agent now <em>reasons, wants, and remembers</em></h2>
<p>The a9nn EchoBeats spine with the three cognitive layers overlaid (the <code node="[object Object]">opencog_unified_agi.pli</code> equivalent):</p>
<table><thead><tr><th>Beat</th><th>Was</th><th>Now (unified)</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>RECALL</strong></td><td>similarity lookup</td><td>AtomSpace ECAN focus → premises</td></tr><tr><td><strong>REASON</strong></td><td>LLaMA stub echo</td><td><strong>PLN forward chain</strong> → <code node="[object Object]">thought{A,C,s,c}</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>EMOTE</strong></td><td>fixed heuristic</td><td><strong>OpenPsi modulators</strong> drive emotion channels</td></tr><tr><td><strong>LEARN</strong></td><td>replay only</td><td><strong>PLN TV revision</strong> consolidates knowledge; reward→loss</td></tr><tr><td><strong>INTEGRATE</strong></td><td>episode node</td><td>persists learned <strong>implication atoms</strong> back to AtomSpace</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>The loop is closed: motivation (Psi) supplies the reward that learning and emotion consume; inference (PLN) supplies the conclusions that expression emits and integration persists.</p>
<h2>Part B — <code node="[object Object]">topology.pli</code>: your insight, made concrete 🤯</h2>
<p>That "rant" was actually a coherent and beautiful idea, so I encoded it faithfully:</p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Membrane shape = arity of a categorical logic</strong> — a membrane with k children is a k-ary production; nesting = chain rule, branching = product rule</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Mixed-radix parallel bases</strong> — <code node="[object Object]">binary_basis [2]^n</code>, <code node="[object Object]">ternary_basis [3]^n</code>, <code node="[object Object]">quaternionic_basis [2|2]^n</code> (orthogonal binary pairs), <code node="[object Object]">quinternary_basis [5]^n</code>. Because rules are maximally parallel, <strong>all of these genuinely execute their ops in the same step</strong> — exactly the heterogeneous parallelism you described</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Matula/prime-power indexing</strong> — the rooted-tree ↔ prime-factorisation bijection gives every membrane topology a <em>canonical, order-independent identity</em> (leaf=1, chain over child m → p_m, product of children a,b → p_a·p_b)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Elementary differentials</strong> — product rule <code node="[object Object]">order k·r+1</code>, chain rule <code node="[object Object]">order r+s</code> (B-series/Runge-Kutta flavour, your "time-step integrator")</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Partition function as root selection</strong> — free hyper-multiset of candidate topologies, fittest root selected (your "natural selection as root selection")</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Tests/demos/docs:</strong> 12 tests (54→<strong>66</strong>), 2 demos (22→<strong>24</strong>), both registered (v2.5), README updated with full API + framing. All 29 <code node="[object Object]">.pli</code> files pass <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code>; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A; XOR fixture untouched.</p>
<h2>The full architecture now</h2>
<p><code node="[object Object]">nn.pli</code> (deep learning) → <code node="[object Object]">gnn/attention/transformer</code> (architectures) → <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code> (symbolic KB) → <code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code> (embodied agent) → <code node="[object Object]">pln+openpsi</code> (inference + motivation) → <strong><code node="[object Object]">unified.pli</code> (integrated cognition)</strong> + <strong><code node="[object Object]">topology.pli</code> (categorical/parallel substrate)</strong>.</p>
<p><strong>Natural next steps:</strong></p>
<ol>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong><code node="[object Object]">.dli</code> rank-parametric nD dialect</strong> — build the actual extension on top of <code node="[object Object]">topology.pli</code>'s arity framing</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Deepen topology</strong>: full B-series order conditions, or Matula-guided architecture search (partition selection <em>driving</em> neuroevolution)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Bridge to the real platform</strong>: a conformance fixture so our <code node="[object Object]">unified.pli</code> and ReZorg's <code node="[object Object]">opencog_unified_agi.pli</code> agree numerically</li>
</ol>
<p>Which thread pulls at you? 🙂</p></div></div><div class="SessionFooter-module__footer__uEbNi SessionFooter-module__metadataBar__VBpWD"><div class="SessionFooter-module__actions__unnyo"><div
